/**
 * Optional download of external images referenced by the repository documents.
 *
 * The default behaviour of RepoSite is to keep remote images as remote links. When the
 * user explicitly opts in, every remote image is fetched through the server side proxy
 * (which performs the SSRF checks) and stored inside the generated site.
 */
import { hashBytes } from './local';
import type { AssetRecord, DocumentPage } from '../schema';

export interface ExternalImageDownloader {
  (url: string): Promise&lt;{
    ok: boolean;
    bytes?: Uint8Array;
    contentType?: string;
    reason?: string;
  }&gt;;
}

export interface ExternalDownloadResult {
  /** assets that were downloaded and stored in the site */
  assets: AssetRecord[];
  /** binary payload per output path */
  binaryAssets: Map&lt;string, Uint8Array&gt;;
  /** documents with the remote URLs rewritten to the local copies */
  documents: DocumentPage[];
  /** images that could not be downloaded, with the reason */
  failed: Array&lt;{ url: string; reason: string }&gt;;
}

export interface ExternalDownloadOptions {
  /** hard limit of downloaded remote images per task */
  maxImages?: number;
  /** bytes of the largest accepted image */
  maxBytes?: number;
  /** hash function used for the file name */
  hash?: (bytes: Uint8Array) =&gt; string;
}

const DEFAULT_MAX_IMAGES = 20;
const DEFAULT_MAX_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024;
const MARKDOWN_IMAGE_PATTERN = /!\[([^\]]*)\]\(\s*([^)\s]+)(?:\s+"[^"]*")?\s*\)/g;

function isRemoteHttpUrl(value: string): boolean {
  return /^https?:\/\//i.test(value.trim());
}

function extensionFor(contentType: string | undefined, url: string): string {
  const fromType = (contentType ?? '').split(';')[0]?.trim().toLowerCase();
  const map: Record&lt;string, string&gt; = {
    'image/png': '.png',
    'image/jpeg': '.jpg',
    'image/jpg': '.jpg',
    'image/gif': '.gif',
    'image/webp': '.webp',
    'image/svg+xml': '.svg',
    'image/avif': '.avif',
    'image/bmp': '.bmp',
    'image/x-icon': '.ico',
  };
  if (fromType &amp;&amp; map[fromType]) return map[fromType];
  try {
    const pathname = new URL(url).pathname;
    const match = /\.[a-z0-9]{1,5}$/i.exec(pathname);
    if (match) return match[0].toLowerCase();
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
  return '.img';
}

function baseNameFor(url: string): string {
  try {
    const pathname = new URL(url).pathname;
    const name = pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean).pop() ?? 'image';
    return name.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9._-]/g, '-').slice(-60) || 'image';
  } catch {
    return 'image';
  }
}

/**
 * Downloads the remote images referenced by the given documents and rewrites the
 * Markdown so the generated site points at the local copies.
 */
export async function downloadExternalImages(
  documents: DocumentPage[],
  imageReferences: Map&lt;string, string[]&gt;,
  downloader: ExternalImageDownloader,
  options: ExternalDownloadOptions = {},
): Promise&lt;ExternalDownloadResult&gt; {
  const maxImages = options.maxImages ?? DEFAULT_MAX_IMAGES;
  const maxBytes = options.maxBytes ?? DEFAULT_MAX_BYTES;
  const hash = options.hash ?? hashBytes;

  const urls: string[] = [];
  for (const references of imageReferences.values()) {
    for (const reference of references) {
      const trimmed = reference.trim();
      if (!isRemoteHttpUrl(trimmed)) continue;</message>

<message type="tool">@@ lines 101-186 of 186 @@
      if (!urls.includes(trimmed)) urls.push(trimmed);
    }
  }

  const assets: AssetRecord[] = [];
  const binaryAssets = new Map&lt;string, Uint8Array&gt;();
  const failed: Array&lt;{ url: string; reason: string }&gt; = [];
  const localPathByUrl = new Map&lt;string, string&gt;();
  const usedHashes = new Set&lt;string&gt;();

  for (const url of urls.slice(0, maxImages)) {
    const result = await downloader(url);
    if (!result.ok || !result.bytes || result.bytes.byteLength === 0) {
      failed.push({ url, reason: result.reason ?? '下载失败' });
      assets.push({
        sourcePath: url,
        type: 'image',
        handling: 'skipped',
        auditStatus: 'warning',
        reason: `外部图片下载失败: ${result.reason ?? '未知原因'}`,
      });
      continue;
    }
    if (result.bytes.byteLength &gt; maxBytes) {
      failed.push({ url, reason: '图片体积超过上限' });
      assets.push({
        sourcePath: url,
        type: 'image',
        handling: 'skipped',
        auditStatus: 'warning',
        reason: '外部图片体积超过上限',
      });
      continue;
    }

    const contentHash = hash(result.bytes);
    if (usedHashes.has(contentHash)) {
      failed.push({ url, reason: '与已下载图片内容重复' });
      continue;
    }
    usedHashes.add(contentHash);

    const extension = extensionFor(result.contentType, url);
    const fileName = `${contentHash}-${baseNameFor(url)}${extension}`.replace(/-+(\.[a-z0-9]+)$/i, '$1');
    const outputPath = `assets/images/${fileName}`;
    binaryAssets.set(outputPath, result.bytes);
    localPathByUrl.set(url, outputPath);
    assets.push({
      sourcePath: url,
      outputPath,
      type: 'image',
      handling: 'copied',
      auditStatus: 'passed',
      bytes: result.bytes.byteLength,
      mimeType: result.contentType ?? 'application/octet-stream',
      reason: '已下载外部图片',
    });
  }

  const rewritten = documents.map((document) =&gt; ({
    ...document,
    contentMarkdown: rewriteRemoteImages(document.contentMarkdown, localPathByUrl),
  }));

  return { assets, binaryAssets, documents: rewritten, failed };
}

/** Rewrites remote image URLs that were downloaded to the local copy. */
export function rewriteRemoteImages(markdown: string, localPathByUrl: Map&lt;string, string&gt;): string {
  if (localPathByUrl.size === 0) return markdown;
  return markdown.replace(MARKDOWN_IMAGE_PATTERN, (full, alt: string, src: string) =&gt; {
    const local = localPathByUrl.get(src.trim());
    if (!local) return full;
    return `![${alt}](${local})`;
  });
}

/** Decodes a base64 payload (as returned by the server side proxy) into bytes. */
export function base64ToBytes(base64: string): Uint8Array {
  const binary = atob(base64);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
  for (let index = 0; index &lt; binary.length; index += 1) {
    bytes[index] = binary.charCodeAt(index);
  }
  return bytes;
}</message>

  const match = classes.find((value) =&gt; value.startsWith('language-'));
  return match ? match.slice('language-'.length).toLowerCase() : 'text';
}

async function loadShiki(): Promise&lt;ShikiHighlighter | null&gt; {
  if (!shikiHighlighterPromise) {
    shikiHighlighterPromise = (async () =&gt; {
      try {
        const [{ createHighlighterCore }, { createJavaScriptRegexEngine }, { bundledThemes }] = await Promise.all([
          import('shiki/core'),
          import('shiki/engine/javascript'),
          import('shiki/themes'),
        ]);
        return (await createHighlighterCore({
          themes: [bundledThemes['github-dark']],
          langs: [],
          engine: createJavaScriptRegexEngine(),
        })) as unknown as ShikiHighlighter;
      } catch {
        return null;
      }
    })();
  }
  return shikiHighlighterPromise;
}

/** Loads a language grammar on demand; unknown languages stay unhighlighted. */
async function ensureLanguage(highlighter: ShikiHighlighter, lang: string): Promise&lt;boolean&gt; {
  const loader = LANGUAGE_LOADERS[lang];
  if (!loader) return false;
  if (!loadedLanguages.has(lang)) {
    try {
      const module = await loader();
      await highlighter.loadLanguage(module.default);
      loadedLanguages.add(lang);
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}

export function walkHast(
  node: HastNode,
  visitor: (node: HastNode, parent?: HastNode) =&gt; void,</message>

import { RepoSiteError } from '../errors';
import type { RepositoryRef } from './url';

/** The only host the app is ever allowed to talk to for repository data. */
export const GITHUB_API_ORIGIN = 'https://api.github.com';

export interface GitHubClientOptions {
  fetchImpl?: typeof fetch;
  /** Optional token, only used server-side to raise the rate limit. Never sent to browsers. */
  token?: string;
  userAgent?: string;
  maxRetries?: number;
  requestTimeoutMs?: number;
}

export interface RepositoryMetadata {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  fullName: string;
  description: string | null;
  htmlUrl: string;
  homepage: string | null;
  defaultBranch: string;
  language: string | null;
  topics: string[];
  stars: number;
  license: { spdxId: string | null; name: string | null } | null;
  archived: boolean;
  pushedAt: string | null;
}

export interface TreeEntry {
  path: string;
  mode: string;
  type: 'blob' | 'tree' | 'commit';
  size: number;
  sha: string;
}

export interface TreeResult {
  entries: TreeEntry[];
  truncated: boolean;
}

export interface ReadmeFile {
  path: string;
  content: string;
}

const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 20_000;

export class GitHubClient {
  private readonly fetchImpl: typeof fetch;
  private readonly token?: string;
  private readonly userAgent: string;
  private readonly maxRetries: number;
  private readonly requestTimeoutMs: number;

  constructor(options: GitHubClientOptions = {}) {
    this.fetchImpl = options.fetchImpl ?? globalThis.fetch.bind(globalThis);
    this.token = options.token;
    this.userAgent = options.userAgent ?? 'RepoSite/1.1 (+https://github.com)';
    this.maxRetries = options.maxRetries ?? 2;
    this.requestTimeoutMs = options.requestTimeoutMs ?? DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS;
  }

  async getRepository(ref: RepositoryRef): Promise&lt;RepositoryMetadata&gt; {
    const data = await this.request&lt;Record&lt;string, unknown&gt;&gt;(
      `/repos/${encodeURIComponent(ref.owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(ref.repo)}`,
    );
    return {
      id: Number(data.id ?? 0),
      name: String(data.name ?? ref.repo),
      fullName: String(data.full_name ?? `${ref.owner}/${ref.repo}`),
      description: typeof data.description === 'string' ? data.description : null,
      htmlUrl: String(data.html_url ?? `https://github.com/${ref.owner}/${ref.repo}`),
      homepage: typeof data.homepage === 'string' ? data.homepage : null,
      defaultBranch: String(data.default_branch ?? 'main'),
      language: typeof data.language === 'string' ? data.language : null,
      topics: Array.isArray(data.topics) ? (data.topics as string[]).slice(0, 30) : [],</message>

  const linkInputs = documents.map((document) =&gt; ({
    slug: document.slug,
    sourcePath: document.sourcePath,
    links: extractMarkdownLinks(document.contentMarkdown),
  }));

  const knownRoutes = new Set&lt;string&gt;(['/', '/docs/', '/404.html']);
  for (const document of documents) knownRoutes.add(`/docs/${document.slug}.html`);

  const linkIssues = auditInternalLinks(linkInputs, {
    knownSourcePaths: new Set(
      documents.map((document) =&gt; document.sourcePath).filter((path): path is string =&gt; Boolean(path)),
    ),
    knownSlugs: new Set(documents.map((document) =&gt; document.slug)),
    knownRoutes,
  });

  const imageIssues = [];
  for (const asset of content.assets) {
    if (asset.handling === 'skipped' &amp;&amp; asset.auditStatus === 'failed') {
      imageIssues.push({
        id: 'image-missing',
        severity: 'blocking' as const,
        category: 'image' as const,
        message: `图片无法解析: ${asset.sourcePath ?? '未知路径'}`,
        target: asset.sourcePath,
        hint: '请修复引用路径或跳过该图片。',
      });
    } else if (asset.handling === 'skipped' &amp;&amp; asset.auditStatus === 'warning') {
      imageIssues.push({
        id: 'image-skipped',
        severity: 'warning' as const,
        category: 'image' as const,
        message: `图片被跳过: ${asset.sourcePath ?? '未知路径'} (${asset.reason ?? '原因未知'})`,
        target: asset.sourcePath,
      });
    }
  }

  return runAudit({
    metadata: content.metadata,
    documents,
    assets: content.assets,
    license: content.license,
    linkIssues,
    imageIssues,
    externalLinkCount: 0,
  });
}

async function resolveLicense(
  entries: TreeEntry[],
  entryByPath: Map&lt;string, TreeEntry&gt;,
  client: GitHubClient,
  reference: { owner: string; repo: string },
  repository: { license?: { spdxId: string | null; name: string | null } | null },
): Promise&lt;LicenseInfo&gt; {
  const detected = detectLicense(entries.map((entry) =&gt; entry.path));
  if (detected.sourcePath) {
    const entry = entryByPath.get(detected.sourcePath);
    const text = entry ? await readText(client, reference, entry) : '';
    const identifier = identifyLicense(text);
    return {
      status: identifier ? 'detected' : 'uncertain',
      sourcePath: detected.sourcePath,
      identifier,
    };
  }

  const spdx = repository.license?.spdxId;
  if (spdx &amp;&amp; spdx !== 'NOASSERTION') {
    return { status: 'detected', identifier: spdx };
  }
  return { status: 'missing' };
}

async function readText(
  client: GitHubClient,
  reference: { owner: string; repo: string },
  entry: { path: string; sha: string },
): Promise&lt;string&gt; {
  const bytes = await client.getBlobBytes(reference, entry.sha);
  return new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: false }).decode(bytes);
}

function pickDocumentPaths(paths: string[], readmePath?: string): string[] {
  const selected: string[] = [];
  for (const path of paths) {
    if (!/\.md$/i.test(path)) continue;
    if (readmePath &amp;&amp; path === readmePath) continue;
    const segments = path.split('/');
    if (segments.length === 1) {
      if (ROOT_DOC_NAMES.includes(path.toLowerCase())) selected.push(path);
      continue;
    }
    if (segments.length &lt;= 3 &amp;&amp; DOC_DIRECTORIES.includes(segments[0]!.toLowerCase())) {
      selected.push(path);
    }
  }
  return selected;</message>

function documentSlug(path: string, readmePath?: string): string {
  if (readmePath &amp;&amp; path === readmePath) return 'readme';
  return pathToSlug(path.replace(/\.md$/i, ''));
}

function extractTitle(markdown: string, path: string): string {
  const heading = /^#\s+(.+)$/m.exec(markdown);
  if (heading) return heading[1]!.trim().slice(0, 200);
  return slugToTitle(path.replace(/\.md$/i, ''));
}

function extractSummary(markdown: string): string {
  const withoutCode = markdown.replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, ' ').replace(/^#+\s+.*$/gm, ' ');
  const text = withoutCode.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  return text.slice(0, 220);
}

export function extractImageReferences(markdown: string): string[] {
  const references: string[] = [];
  const pattern = /!\[[^\]]*\]\(([^)\s]+)(?:\s+"[^"]*")?\)/g;
  let match = pattern.exec(markdown);
  while (match) {
    references.push(match[1]!);
    match = pattern.exec(markdown);
  }
  const htmlPattern = /&lt;img[^&gt;]+src\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"/gi;
  let htmlMatch = htmlPattern.exec(markdown);
  while (htmlMatch) {
    references.push(htmlMatch[1]!);
    htmlMatch = htmlPattern.exec(markdown);
  }
  return references;
}

function summarizeTree(paths: string[]): string {
  const directories = new Map&lt;string, number&gt;();
  for (const path of paths) {
    const segments = path.split('/');
    for (let index = 1; index &lt; segments.length; index += 1) {
      const directory = segments.slice(0, index).join('/');
      directories.set(directory, (directories.get(directory) ?? 0) + 1);
    }
  }
  return [...directories.entries()]
    .sort((a, b) =&gt; a[0].localeCompare(b[0]))
    .slice(0, 200)
    .map(([directory, count]) =&gt; `${directory}/ (${count} files)`)
    .join('\n');
}

function extensionOf(path: string): string {
  const index = path.lastIndexOf('.');
  return index === -1 ? '' : path.slice(index).toLowerCase();
}

function baseNameOf(path: string): string {
  return path.split('/').pop() ?? path;
}

function sanitizeFileName(name: string): string {
  return (
    name
      .replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9._-]+/g, '-')
      .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '')
      .slice(0, 60) || 'asset'
  );
}</message>

import { RepoSiteError } from '../errors';

const EXCLUDED_SEGMENTS = new Set([
  '.git',
  'node_modules',
  'dist',
  'build',
  'out',
  'coverage',
  '.next',
  '.nuxt',
  '.cache',
  '.venv',
  'venv',
  '__pycache__',
  '.idea',
  '.vscode',
]);

const IMAGE_EXTENSIONS = new Set([
  '.png',
  '.jpg',
  '.jpeg',
  '.gif',
  '.webp',
  '.avif',
  '.bmp',
  '.ico',
  '.svg',
  '.apng',
]);

const MARKDOWN_EXTENSIONS = new Set(['.md', '.markdown', '.mdx']);

/**
 * Normalizes a repository path to a safe POSIX relative path.
 * Rejects traversal, absolute paths, backslashes and control characters.
 */
export function normalizeRepoPath(input: string): string {
  const raw = (input ?? '').trim();
  if (!raw) {
    throw new RepoSiteError('INVALID_PATH', '路径不能为空。');
  }
  if (raw.includes('\\')) {
    throw new RepoSiteError('INVALID_PATH', `路径不允许使用反斜杠: ${raw}`);
  }
  if (raw.includes('\0') || /[\u0000-\u001f]/.test(raw)) {
    throw new RepoSiteError('INVALID_PATH', '路径包含非法控制字符。');
  }
  if (raw.startsWith('/')) {
    throw new RepoSiteError('PATH_TRAVERSAL', `不允许使用绝对路径: ${raw}`);
  }
  if (/^[A-Za-z]:/.test(raw)) {
    throw new RepoSiteError('PATH_TRAVERSAL', `不允许使用驱动器路径: ${raw}`);
  }

  const segments: string[] = [];
  for (const segment of raw.split('/')) {
    if (segment === '' || segment === '.') continue;
    if (segment === '..') {
      throw new RepoSiteError('PATH_TRAVERSAL', `不允许使用目录穿越: ${raw}`);
    }
    segments.push(segment);
  }
  if (segments.length === 0) {
    throw new RepoSiteError('INVALID_PATH', '路径不能为空。');
  }
  return segments.join('/');
}

/** Resolves a relative reference (e.g. "./img/a.png") against the directory of a document. */
export function resolveRelativePath(baseDir: string, reference: string): string {
  const ref = reference.trim();
  if (!ref) {
    throw new RepoSiteError('INVALID_PATH', '引用路径不能为空。');
  }
  if (/^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/i.test(ref) || ref.startsWith('//') || ref.startsWith('#')) {
    throw new RepoSiteError('INVALID_PATH', `引用不是仓库内相对路径: ${reference}`);
  }
  const base = baseDir ? baseDir.split('/').filter(Boolean) : [];
  const combined = [...base, ...ref.split('/')].filter((part) =&gt; part !== '' &amp;&amp; part !== '.');

  const stack: string[] = [];
  for (const part of combined) {
    if (part === '..') {
      if (stack.length === 0) {
        throw new RepoSiteError('PATH_TRAVERSAL', `引用超出仓库根目录: ${reference}`);
      }
      stack.pop();
      continue;
    }
    stack.push(part);
  }
  if (stack.length === 0) {
    throw new RepoSiteError('INVALID_PATH', `引用路径无效: ${reference}`);
  }
  return normalizeRepoPath(stack.join('/'));
}

export function dirnameOf(path: string): string {</message>

  const normalized = normalizeRepoPath(path);
  const index = normalized.lastIndexOf('/');
  return index === -1 ? '' : normalized.slice(0, index);
}

export function basenameOf(path: string): string {
  const normalized = normalizeRepoPath(path);
  const index = normalized.lastIndexOf('/');
  return index === -1 ? normalized : normalized.slice(index + 1);
}

export function extensionOf(path: string): string {
  const name = basenameOf(path).toLowerCase();
  const index = name.lastIndexOf('.');
  return index === -1 ? '' : name.slice(index);
}

export function isImagePath(path: string): boolean {
  return IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.has(extensionOf(path));
}

export function isMarkdownPath(path: string): boolean {
  return MARKDOWN_EXTENSIONS.has(extensionOf(path));
}

export function isExcludedPath(path: string): boolean {
  return path.split('/').some((segment) =&gt; EXCLUDED_SEGMENTS.has(segment));
}

/** Converts a repository path into a stable, URL-safe document slug. */
export function pathToSlug(path: string): string {
  const normalized = normalizeRepoPath(path);
  const withoutExtension = normalized.replace(/\.(md|markdown|mdx)$/i, '');
  const slug = withoutExtension
    .split('/')
    .map((segment) =&gt;
      segment
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9._-]+/g, '-')
        .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, ''),
    )
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join('/');
  return slug || 'index';
}

export function slugToTitle(slug: string): string {
  const last = slug.split('/').pop() ?? slug;
  const cleaned = last.replace(/[-_]+/g, ' ').trim();
  return cleaned.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + cleaned.slice(1);
}</message>

import { RepoSiteError } from '../errors';
import { dirnameOf, isMarkdownPath, normalizeRepoPath, resolveRelativePath } from '../github/paths';
import type { AuditIssue } from '../schema';
import type { MarkdownLink } from './markdown';

export interface LinkAuditInput {
  /** slug of the document that contains the link */
  slug: string;
  /** repository path of the source document, when it comes from the repository */
  sourcePath?: string;
  links: MarkdownLink[];
}

export interface LinkAuditContext {
  /** normalized repository paths of all known documents */
  knownSourcePaths: Set&lt;string&gt;;
  /** slugs of all generated pages */
  knownSlugs: Set&lt;string&gt;;
  /** routes published by the generator */
  knownRoutes: Set&lt;string&gt;;
}

/**
 * Checks internal links against the known documents. Links that cannot be resolved
 * are reported as blocking issues so the user can fix or remove them.
 */
export function auditInternalLinks(
  inputs: LinkAuditInput[],
  context: LinkAuditContext,
): AuditIssue[] {
  const issues: AuditIssue[] = [];

  for (const input of inputs) {
    for (const link of input.links) {
      if (link.kind !== 'internal') continue;
      const target = stripQueryAndHash(link.href);
      if (!target) continue;

      if (target.startsWith('/')) {
        if (!context.knownRoutes.has(target)) {
          issues.push({
            id: 'link-missing-route',
            severity: 'blocking',
            category: 'link',
            message: `内部链接指向不存在的页面: ${target}`,
            target: `${input.slug} → ${target}`,
            hint: '请修正链接或移除该引用。',
          });
        }
        continue;
      }

      if (isMarkdownPath(target)) {
        const resolved = resolveTarget(input.sourcePath, target);
        if (resolved &amp;&amp; !context.knownSourcePaths.has(resolved)) {
          issues.push({
            id: 'link-missing-document',
            severity: 'blocking',
            category: 'link',
            message: `内部文档链接指向不存在的文件: ${target}`,
            target: `${input.slug} → ${target}`,
            hint: '请修正链接或移除该引用。',
          });
        }
        continue;
      }

      const slugMatch = [...context.knownSlugs].some(
        (slug) =&gt; slug === target || slug.endsWith(`/${target}`),
      );
      const routeMatch = [...context.knownRoutes].some((route) =&gt; route.endsWith(target));
      if (!slugMatch &amp;&amp; !routeMatch) {
        issues.push({
          id: 'link-unresolved',
          severity: 'blocking',
          category: 'link',
          message: `内部链接无法解析到已生成页面: ${target}`,
          target: `${input.slug} → ${target}`,
          hint: '请修正链接或移除该引用。',
        });
      }
    }
  }

  return issues;
}

function resolveTarget(sourcePath: string | undefined, target: string): string | undefined {
  try {
    if (!sourcePath) return normalizeRepoPath(target);
    return resolveRelativePath(dirnameOf(sourcePath), target);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof RepoSiteError) return undefined;
    throw error;
  }
}

export function stripQueryAndHash(href: string): string {
  return href.split('#')[0]!.split('?')[0]!.trim();
}</message>

  visitor: (node: HastNode, parent?: HastNode) =&gt; void,
  parent?: HastNode,
): void {
  visitor(node, parent);
  for (const child of node.children ?? []) {
    walkHast(child, visitor, node);
  }
}

function textContent(node: HastNode): string {
  if (node.type === 'text') return node.value ?? '';
  return (node.children ?? []).map((child) =&gt; textContent(child)).join('');
}

/** Small HTML to text reducer used for llms.txt and search summaries. */
export function htmlToPlainText(html: string): string {
  return html
    .replace(/&lt;script[\s\S]*?&lt;\/script&gt;/gi, '')
    .replace(/&lt;style[\s\S]*?&lt;\/style&gt;/gi, '')
    .replace(/&lt;\/(p|div|section|article|h[1-6]|li|tr|pre|blockquote)&gt;/gi, '\n')
    .replace(/&lt;br\s*\/?&gt;/gi, '\n')
    .replace(/&lt;[^&gt;]+&gt;/g, '')
    .replace(/&amp;lt;/g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/&amp;gt;/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/&amp;quot;/g, '"')
    .replace(/&amp;#39;/g, "'")
    .replace(/&amp;amp;/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n')
    .trim();
}

export function escapeHtml(input: string): string {
  return input
    .replace(/&amp;/g, '&amp;amp;')
    .replace(/&lt;/g, '&amp;lt;')
    .replace(/&gt;/g, '&amp;gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&amp;quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&amp;#39;');
}

/** Plain-text summary of a Markdown document (headings + first paragraph). */
export function markdownToPlainText(markdown: string, maxLength = 600): string {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  let length = 0;
  for (const rawLine of (markdown ?? '').split(/\r?\n/)) {
    const line = rawLine.trim();
    if (!line) continue;
    if (line.startsWith('```') || line.startsWith('~~~')) continue;
    if (/^!\[/.test(line)) continue;
    const cleaned = line
      .replace(/^#{1,6}\s*/, '')
      .replace(/^&gt;\s*/, '')
      .replace(/^[-*+]\s+/, '')
      .replace(/^\d+\.\s+/, '')
      .replace(/\[([^\]]*)\]\([^)]*\)/g, '$1')
      .replace(/`([^`]*)`/g, '$1')
      .replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '$1')
      .replace(/__([^_]+)__/g, '$1')
      .replace(/[*_]{1,3}([^*_]+)[*_]{1,3}/g, '$1')
      .trim();
    if (!cleaned) continue;
    lines.push(cleaned);
    length += cleaned.length;
    if (length &gt; maxLength) break;
  }
  const text = lines.join(' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  return text.length &gt; maxLength ? `${text.slice(0, maxLength - 1)}…` : text;
}</message>

/** Lightweight link extraction used by the link audit (no HTML rendering required). */
export function extractMarkdownLinks(markdown: string): MarkdownLink[] {
  const links: MarkdownLink[] = [];
  const pattern = /(!?)\[([^\]]*)\]\(([^)\s]+)(?:\s+"[^"]*")?\)/g;
  let match = pattern.exec(markdown);
  while (match) {
    const href = match[3] ?? '';
    links.push({ href, text: match[2] ?? '', kind: classifyLink(href) });
    match = pattern.exec(markdown);
  }
  return links;
}

function rehypeShiki(options: { enabled: boolean }) {
  return async (tree: HastNode) =&gt; {
    if (!options.enabled) return tree;
    const highlighter = await loadShiki();
    if (!highlighter) return tree;

    const blocks: Array&lt;{ node: HastNode; parent: HastNode; code: HastNode }&gt; = [];
    walkHast(tree, (node, parent) =&gt; {
      if (!parent || node.type !== 'element' || node.tagName !== 'pre') return;
      const code = node.children?.find((child) =&gt; child.type === 'element' &amp;&amp; child.tagName === 'code');
      if (!code) return;
      blocks.push({ node, parent, code });
    });

    for (const block of blocks) {
      const source = textContent(block.code);
      if (!source.trim()) continue;
      const lang = detectLanguage(block.code);
      if (!(await ensureLanguage(highlighter, lang))) continue;
      try {
        const highlighted = highlighter.codeToHtml(source, {
          lang,
          theme: 'github-dark',
        });
        block.parent.children = (block.parent.children ?? []).map((child) =&gt;
          child === block.node ? { type: 'raw', value: highlighted } : child,
        );
      } catch {
        // Unknown language: keep the sanitized plain code block.
      }
    }

    return tree;
  };
}

  const mdast = processor.parse(markdown ?? '');
  const hast = (await processor.run(mdast)) as unknown as HastNode;
  const html = String(await processor.stringify(hast as never));

  const headings: Heading[] = [];
  const links: MarkdownLink[] = [];
  const images: MarkdownImage[] = [];

  walkHast(hast, (node) =&gt; {
    if (node.type !== 'element' || !node.tagName) return;
    const tag = node.tagName;
    if (/^h[1-6]$/.test(tag)) {
      headings.push({
        id: String(node.properties?.id ?? ''),
        text: textContent(node),
        depth: Number(tag.slice(1)),
      });
    }
    if (tag === 'a') {
      const href = String(node.properties?.href ?? '');
      links.push({ href, text: textContent(node), kind: classifyLink(href) });
    }
    if (tag === 'img') {
      images.push({ src: String(node.properties?.src ?? ''), alt: String(node.properties?.alt ?? '') });
    }
  });

  return { html, headings, links, images, plainText: htmlToPlainText(html) };
}

function classifyLink(href: string): MarkdownLink['kind'] {
  if (!href) return 'internal';
  if (href.startsWith('#')) return 'anchor';
  if (/^mailto:/i.test(href)) return 'mail';
  if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(href)) return 'external';
  return 'internal';
}</message>

import JSZip from 'jszip';
import type { GeneratedFile } from '../site/generator';

export interface ZipPackageResult {
  blob: Blob;
  fileCount: number;
  totalBytes: number;
  fileNames: string[];
}

export const SECRET_PATTERNS: Array&lt;{ name: string; pattern: RegExp }&gt; = [
  { name: 'openai-style-key', pattern: /\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}/ },
  { name: 'bearer-token', pattern: /\bBearer\s+[A-Za-z0-9._~+/-]{16,}=*/ },
  { name: 'api-key-assignment', pattern: /\b(?:api[_-]?key|apikey|access[_-]?token)\b\s*[:=]\s*["']?[A-Za-z0-9._~+/-]{16,}/i },
  { name: 'server-key-env', pattern: /\b(?:DEEPSEEK|MINIMAX)_API_KEY\s*=/ },
  { name: 'authorization-header', pattern: /["']authorization["']\s*:\s*["'][^"']{12,}/i },
];

/** Returns the names of files that look like they contain credentials. */
export function scanForSecrets(files: Array&lt;{ path: string; content: string | Uint8Array }&gt;): string[] {
  const suspicious: string[] = [];
  for (const file of files) {
    if (typeof file.content !== 'string') continue;
    for (const { name, pattern } of SECRET_PATTERNS) {
      if (pattern.test(file.content)) {
        suspicious.push(`${file.path} (${name})`);
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  return suspicious;
}

/**
 * Packages the generated site into a ZIP archive. The archive is rejected when a file
 * looks like it contains a credential.
 */
export async function packageSite(
  files: GeneratedFile[],
  options: { maxBytes?: number } = {},
): Promise&lt;ZipPackageResult&gt; {
  const maxBytes = options.maxBytes ?? 64 * 1024 * 1024;
  const totalBytes = files.reduce(
    (sum, file) =&gt; sum + (typeof file.content === 'string' ? file.content.length : file.content.byteLength),
    0,
  );

  if (totalBytes &gt; maxBytes) {
    throw new Error(
      `生成内容约 ${Math.round(totalBytes / 1024 / 1024)} MB，超过浏览器可承受的打包上限 (${Math.round(maxBytes / 1024 / 1024)} MB)。`,
    );
  }

  const suspicious = scanForSecrets(files);
  if (suspicious.length &gt; 0) {
    throw new Error(`检测到疑似密钥内容，已阻止导出: ${suspicious.join(', ')}`);
  }

  const zip = new JSZip();
  for (const file of files) {
    if (typeof file.content === 'string') {
      zip.file(file.path, file.content);
    } else {
      zip.file(file.path, file.content as unknown as Uint8Array);
    }
  }

  const blob = await zip.generateAsync({ type: 'blob', compression: 'DEFLATE', compressionOptions: { level: 6 } });
  return {
    blob,
    fileCount: files.length,
    totalBytes: blob.size,
    fileNames: files.map((file) =&gt; file.path),
  };
}</message>

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Eraser, Save } from 'lucide-react';

import { Alert, Button, Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, Input, Label, Separator } from './ui/primitives';
import { clearSettings, loadSettings, saveSettings } from '@/lib/settings';
import type { AIKeyMode, AIProviderName, GenerationLanguage } from '@/lib/schema';

export default function ConfigPanel() {
  const [provider, setProvider] = useState&lt;AIProviderName&gt;('deepseek');
  const [keyMode, setKeyMode] = useState&lt;AIKeyMode&gt;('server');
  const [language, setLanguage] = useState&lt;GenerationLanguage&gt;('zh-CN');
  const [downloadExternalImages, setDownloadExternalImages] = useState(false);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() =&gt; {
    const settings = loadSettings();
    setProvider(settings.preferredProvider);
    setKeyMode(settings.preferredKeyMode);
    setLanguage(settings.targetLanguage);
    setDownloadExternalImages(settings.downloadExternalImages);
  }, []);

  const handleSave = () =&gt; {
    saveSettings({ provider, keyMode, language, downloadExternalImages } as never);
    setSaved(true);
  };

  const handleClear = () =&gt; {
    clearSettings();
    setProvider('deepseek');
    setKeyMode('server');
    setLanguage('zh-CN');
    setDownloadExternalImages(false);
    setSaved(false);
  };

  return (
    &lt;div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-3xl flex-col gap-6 px-4 py-8"&gt;
      &lt;Card&gt;
        &lt;CardHeader&gt;
          &lt;CardTitle&gt;AI 与生成偏好&lt;/CardTitle&gt;
        &lt;/CardHeader&gt;
        &lt;CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-4"&gt;
          &lt;Alert tone="info"&gt;
            这里只保存非敏感偏好。API Key 永远不会写入本地存储、URL、日志或导出的 ZIP；使用自带 Key 时，Key 只存在于当前页面的内存中。
          &lt;/Alert&gt;

          &lt;div className="flex flex-col gap-2"&gt;
            &lt;Label htmlFor="cfg-provider"&gt;AI 服务商&lt;/Label&gt;
            &lt;select
              id="cfg-provider"
              className="h-9 rounded-md border border-input bg-transparent px-2 text-sm"

import { renderMarkdown, markdownToPlainText, escapeHtml } from '../content/markdown';
import { dirnameOf, resolveRelativePath } from '../github/paths';
import type {
  AssetRecord,
  AuditReport,
  DocumentPage,
  GenerationLanguage,
  ProjectMetadata,
} from '../schema';
import {
  renderDocIndex,
  renderDocument,
  renderHome,
  renderNotFound,
  SITE_CSS,
  SITE_JS,
  type NavItem,
} from './templates';

export interface SiteInput {
  metadata: ProjectMetadata;
  documents: DocumentPage[];
  assets: AssetRecord[];
  binaryAssets?: Map&lt;string, Uint8Array&gt;;
  language: GenerationLanguage;
  summary?: string;
  highlights?: string[];
  quickStart?: string;
  tags?: string[];
  audience?: string;
  siteOrigin?: string;
  audit?: AuditReport;
}

export interface GeneratedFile {
  path: string;
  content: string | Uint8Array;
}

export interface GeneratedSite {
  files: GeneratedFile[];
  totalBytes: number;
  fileCount: number;
}

const CHUNK_SIZE = 1200;

export async function generateSite(input: SiteInput): Promise&lt;GeneratedSite&gt; {
  const { metadata, documents, language } = input;
  const files: GeneratedFile[] = [];

  const ordered = [...documents].sort((a, b) =&gt; a.order - b.order || a.title.localeCompare(b.title));
  const nav: NavItem[] = ordered.map((document) =&gt; ({
    title: document.title,
    href: `/docs/${document.slug}.html`,
    slug: document.slug,
  }));

  const assetUrlByPath = new Map&lt;string, string&gt;();
  for (const asset of input.assets) {
    if (asset.handling === 'copied' &amp;&amp; asset.outputPath &amp;&amp; asset.sourcePath) {
      assetUrlByPath.set(asset.sourcePath, `/${asset.outputPath}`);
    }
  }

  const pagePathBySourcePath = new Map&lt;string, string&gt;();
  for (const document of ordered) {
    if (document.sourcePath) pagePathBySourcePath.set(document.sourcePath, `/docs/${document.slug}.html`);
  }

  const siteTitle = metadata.name;
  const siteDescription = metadata.description || markdownToPlainText(metadata.description, 200);
  const baseOptions = {
    metadata,
    language,
    nav,
    siteTitle,
    siteDescription,
    canonicalPath: '/',
  };

  // --- Documentation pages -------------------------------------------------
  const pageSummaries: Array&lt;{ title: string; href: string; summary: string; sourcePath?: string }&gt; = [];

  for (const document of ordered) {
    const markdown = rewriteDocument(
      document.contentMarkdown,
      document.sourcePath,
      assetUrlByPath,
      pagePathBySourcePath,
      metadata,
    );
    const rendered = await renderMarkdown(markdown, { highlight: true });

    const html = renderDocument({
      ...baseOptions,
      canonicalPath: `/docs/${document.slug}.html`,
      title: document.title,
      html: rendered.html,
      headings: rendered.headings,
      sourcePath: document.sourcePath,
      origin: document.origin,
    });
    files.push({ path: `docs/${document.slug}.html`, content: html });
    files.push({ path: `markdown/${document.slug}.md`, content: markdown });

    pageSummaries.push({
      title: document.title,
      href: `/docs/${document.slug}.html`,
      summary: document.summary || markdownToPlainText(markdown, 220),
      sourcePath: document.sourcePath,
    });
  }

  // --- Home page -----------------------------------------------------------
  const homeMarkdown = buildHomeMarkdown(input);
  const homeRendered = await renderMarkdown(homeMarkdown, { highlight: true });
  const homeHtml = renderHome({
    ...baseOptions,
    canonicalPath: '/',</message>

    summary: input.summary || markdownToPlainText(homeMarkdown, 800),
    highlights: input.highlights ?? [],
    quickStart: input.quickStart ?? '',

    audience: input.audience,
    sourceCommit: metadata.sourceCommitSha,
  });
  files.push({ path: 'index.html', content: homeHtml });
  files.push({ path: 'markdown/README.md', content: homeMarkdown });

  // --- Docs index ----------------------------------------------------------
  files.push({
    path: 'docs/index.html',
    content: renderDocIndex({
      ...baseOptions,
      canonicalPath: '/docs/',
      items: pageSummaries,
    }),
  });

  // --- 404 -----------------------------------------------------------------
  files.push({
    path: '404.html',
    content: renderNotFound({ ...baseOptions, canonicalPath: '/404.html' }),
  });

  // --- Static assets -------------------------------------------------------
  files.push({ path: 'assets/css/site.css', content: SITE_CSS });
  files.push({ path: 'assets/js/site.js', content: SITE_JS });
  for (const [path, bytes] of input.binaryAssets ?? new Map&lt;string, Uint8Array&gt;()) {
    files.push({ path, content: bytes });
  }

  // --- AI friendly outputs -------------------------------------------------
  files.push({ path: 'ai/manifest.json', content: buildManifest(input, ordered) });
  files.push({ path: 'ai/pages.json', content: buildPagesJson(input, ordered) });
  files.push({ path: 'ai/chunks.json', content: buildChunksJson(input, ordered) });
  files.push({ path: 'llms.txt', content: buildLlmsTxt(input, ordered, pageSummaries) });
  files.push({ path: 'llms-full.txt', content: buildLlmsFullTxt(input, ordered) });
  files.push({ path: 'robots.txt', content: buildRobotsTxt(input.siteOrigin) });
  files.push({ path: 'sitemap.xml', content: buildSitemap(input, ordered, pageSummaries) });

  const totalBytes = files.reduce((sum, file) =&gt; sum + byteLength(file.content), 0);
  return { files, totalBytes, fileCount: files.length };
}

function byteLength(content: string | Uint8Array): number {
  if (typeof content === 'string') return new TextEncoder().encode(content).length;
  return content.byteLength;
}

/** Rewrites image and repository-file references so the generated site is self contained. */
export function rewriteDocument(
  markdown: string,
  sourcePath: string | undefined,
  assetUrlByPath: Map&lt;string, string&gt;,
  pagePathBySourcePath: Map&lt;string, string&gt;,
  metadata: ProjectMetadata,
): string {
  let output = markdown;

  output = output.replace(/!\[([^\]]*)\]\(([^)\s]+)(?:\s+"[^"]*")?\)/g, (match, alt: string, src: string) =&gt; {
    if (/^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/i.test(src) || src.startsWith('//') || src.startsWith('#')) return match;
    const resolved = resolveMaybe(sourcePath, src);
    if (!resolved) return match;
    const url = assetUrlByPath.get(resolved);
    if (!url) return match;
    return `![${alt}](${url})`;
  });

  output = output.replace(/&lt;img\s+([^&gt;]*?)src\s*=\s*"([^"]*)"([^&gt;]*)&gt;/gi, (match, before: string, src: string, after: string) =&gt; {
    if (/^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/i.test(src) || src.startsWith('//') || src.startsWith('#')) return match;
    const resolved = resolveMaybe(sourcePath, src);
    if (!resolved) return match;
    const url = assetUrlByPath.get(resolved);
    if (!url) return match;
    return `&lt;img ${before}src="${url}"${after}&gt;`;
  });

  output = output.replace(/\[([^\]]*)\]\(([^)\s]+)(?:\s+"[^"]*")?\)/g, (match, label: string, href: string) =&gt; {
    if (/^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/i.test(href) || href.startsWith('//') || href.startsWith('#')) return match;
    const resolved = resolveMaybe(sourcePath, href);
    if (!resolved) return match;
    if (pagePathBySourcePath.has(resolved)) {
      return `[${label}](${pagePathBySourcePath.get(resolved)})`;
    }
    if (/\.md$/i.test(resolved)) {
      const base = metadata.repositoryUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '');
      return `[${label}](${base}/blob/${metadata.sourceCommitSha}/${resolved})`;
    }
    return match;
  });

  return output;
}

function resolveMaybe(sourcePath: string | undefined, reference: string): string | undefined {
  const clean = reference.split('#')[0]!.split('?')[0]!;
  if (!clean) return undefined;
  try {
    return resolveRelativePath(sourcePath ? dirnameOf(sourcePath) : '', clean);
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  }
}

    lines.push('');
  }
  if (input.quickStart) {
    lines.push('## Quick start', '', '```bash', input.quickStart, '```', '');
  }
  lines.push('## Source', '');
  lines.push(`- Repository: ${metadata.repositoryUrl}`);
  lines.push(`- Commit: ${metadata.sourceCommitSha}`);
  if (metadata.license?.identifier) {</message>

import { z } from 'zod';

export const localSettingsSchema = z.object({
  preferredProvider: z.enum(['deepseek', 'minimax']).default('deepseek'),
  preferredKeyMode: z.enum(['server', 'byok']).default('server'),
  targetLanguage: z.enum(['zh-CN', 'en-US']).default('zh-CN'),
  downloadExternalImages: z.boolean().default(false),
  preferredTheme: z.enum(['light', 'dark', 'system']).optional(),
});

export type LocalSettings = z.infer&lt;typeof localSettingsSchema&gt;;

export const SETTINGS_STORAGE_KEY = 'reposite.settings';

export function isBrowser(): boolean {
  return typeof window !== 'undefined' &amp;&amp; typeof window.localStorage !== 'undefined';
}

/** Loads the non-sensitive preferences. API keys are never stored here. */
export function loadSettings(storage?: Storage): LocalSettings {
  const store = storage ?? (isBrowser() ? window.localStorage : undefined);
  if (!store) return localSettingsSchema.parse({});
  try {
    const raw = store.getItem(SETTINGS_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return localSettingsSchema.parse({});
    const parsed = localSettingsSchema.safeParse(JSON.parse(raw));
    return parsed.success ? parsed.data : localSettingsSchema.parse({});
  } catch {
    return localSettingsSchema.parse({});
  }
}

export function saveSettings(settings: LocalSettings, storage?: Storage): void {
  const store = storage ?? (isBrowser() ? window.localStorage : undefined);
  if (!store) return;
  const safe = localSettingsSchema.parse(settings);
  store.setItem(SETTINGS_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(safe));
}

export function clearSettings(storage?: Storage): void {
  const store = storage ?? (isBrowser() ? window.localStorage : undefined);
  if (!store) return;
  store.removeItem(SETTINGS_STORAGE_KEY);
}

/** Guards against accidental persistence of API keys. */
export function assertNoSecrets(settings: Record&lt;string, unknown&gt;): void {
  const forbidden = ['apikey', 'authorization', 'token', 'secret'];
  for (const key of Object.keys(settings)) {
    if (forbidden.some((name) =&gt; key.toLowerCase().includes(name))) {
      throw new Error(`本地配置不得包含敏感字段: ${key}`);
    }
  }
}</message>

# RepoSite 技术实现文档

**版本：** V1.1  
**项目定位：** 将公开 GitHub 仓库转换为结构清晰、易于人类阅读和 AI 系统检索理解的项目主页与文档网站。

## 1. 项目目标

RepoSite 允许用户输入公开 GitHub 仓库地址，读取仓库中的 README、Markdown 文档、项目元数据和图片，生成规范的静态网站。

核心目标：

- 自动生成项目介绍主页、文档导航和内容页面。
- 优化语义化 HTML、Markdown、JSON 元数据和内容索引，方便搜索引擎及 AI 系统发现、解析和引用项目内容。
- 支持 DeepSeek、MiniMax 两类 AI 服务，允许使用服务端配置的 API Key，或由用户提供自己的 API Key。
- 支持中文或英文生成，每次生成选择一种语言。
- 生成前提供内容预览、图片检查、链接检查、许可证检查和来源审计。
- 用户确认后，使用确定性模板生成静态网站，并打包为 ZIP。
- 不在 V1 中执行仓库中的任意脚本，也不允许 AI 直接生成并执行任意网站代码。

&gt; 说明：RepoSite 能改善内容的可发现性和可解析性，但不能保证第三方 AI 系统一定抓取、索引或用于模型训练。

## 2. 最终决策

| 项目 | 最终方案 |
|---|---|
| 前端技术 | Astro + TypeScript |
| UI | Tailwind CSS + shadcn/ui + React |
| 图标 | Lucide |
| 部署 | Netlify |
| AI 提供商 | DeepSeek、MiniMax |
| AI 使用模式 | 服务端配置与用户自带 API Key 同时支持 |
| 生成语言 | 支持中文、英文选择；单次生成一种语言 |
| 本地图片数量上限 | 50 张 |
| 外部图片 | 默认保留外链，可选下载到本地 |
| 无 LICENSE | 显示警告，用户明确确认后允许继续 |
| 仓库范围 | 仅公开 GitHub 仓库 |
| 配置持久化 | 浏览器 LocalStorage 保存非敏感配置 |
| 账号系统 | V1 不需要 |
| 数据库 | V1 不需要 |
| 生成确认流程 | 预览 → 审核 → 用户确认 → 生成 → ZIP 导出 |
| 发布方式 | 用户自行部署，不提供一键 Netlify 发布 |
| AI 图片生成 | V1 不支持 |

## 3. 系统架构

### 3.1 总体架构

```text
┌───────────────────────────────────────────┐
│              Astro Web App                │
│                                           │
│  仓库输入 / 配置 / 内容预览 / 审核 / 导出  │
└──────────────────┬────────────────────────┘
                   │
          ┌────────┴─────────┐
          ▼                  ▼
┌─────────────────┐  ┌────────────────────┐
│ GitHub Reader   │  │ AI Provider Client │
│                 │  │                    │
│ Repo Metadata   │  │ Server-side Mode   │
│ README / Docs   │  │ User-key Mode      │
│ Tree / Images   │  │ DeepSeek / MiniMax │
└────────┬────────┘  └─────────┬──────────┘
         │                     │
         ▼                     ▼
┌─────────────────┐  ┌────────────────────┐
│ Content Pipeline│  │ Netlify Functions  │
│                 │  │                    │
│ Markdown Parser │  │ Key isolation      │
│ Schema Validate │  │ Request validation │
│ Source Tracking │  │ Rate limiting      │
└────────┬────────┘  └─────────┬──────────┘
         │                     │
         ▼                     ▼
┌───────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Preview &amp; Audit                           │
│                                           │
│ Content / Images / Links / License /      │
│ Sources / Security                        │
└──────────────────┬────────────────────────┘
                   │ 用户确认
                   ▼
┌───────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Deterministic Static Site Generator       │
│                                           │
│ TypeScript Templates + Validated Data     │
│ HTML / CSS / JS / Markdown / JSON         │
└──────────────────┬────────────────────────┘
                   │
                   ▼
             ZIP Export
```

### 3.2 架构原则

1. Astro 负责 RepoSite 自身的界面与静态页面。
2. 用户运行期间输入的仓库数据不能直接变成 Astro 构建时的文件，因此单独实现基于 TypeScript 的静态站点生成器。
3. 生成器只接受通过 Zod 校验的数据模型，并使用固定模板渲染。
4. AI 负责辅助提取、归纳和撰写内容，不负责输出可直接执行的网站程序。
5. GitHub 仓库内容、AI 输出和用户配置均视为不可信输入。
6. AI 请求统一经过 Provider 抽象层，按用户选择切换服务端 Key 或用户自带 Key。
7. 生成前必须完成审核；存在阻断级问题时不能直接进入最终导出。

## 4. 技术选型

| 模块 | 技术方案 |
|---|---|
| 应用框架 | Astro |
| 编程语言 | TypeScript |
| 交互组件 | React |
| UI 样式 | Tailwind CSS |
| 组件库 | shadcn/ui |
| 图标库 | lucide-react |
| GitHub 数据 | GitHub REST API |
| Markdown | unified、remark、rehype |
| HTML 安全处理 | rehype-sanitize |
| 代码高亮 | Shiki |
| 数据校验 | Zod |
| ZIP 打包 | JSZip |
| AI | DeepSeek、MiniMax |</message>

  directory = "netlify/functions"
```

具体函数目录、运行时和 Node.js 版本应以项目选定的 Netlify 配置为准。部署前验证构建、函数调用、超时和环境变量是否正确。

## 14. 安全策略

### 14.1 输入安全

- 严格校验 GitHub 仓库 URL。
- 固定 GitHub API 主机，不接受任意 API 地址。
- 规范化文件路径，阻止目录穿越。
- 对仓库内容、AI 输出和用户编辑内容分别校验。
- 限制文件大小、输入字节数、输出 Token 和请求时间。

### 14.2 XSS 与主动内容

- Markdown 通过安全管线处理。
- HTML 输出使用转义和允许列表。
- SVG 经过净化。
- 不允许未经检查的内联脚本。
- 使用合适的 Content Security Policy。
- 用户可编辑的项目介绍也必须经过相同的安全校验。

### 14.3 API Key 安全

- 服务端 Key 仅存储在部署环境变量中。
- BYOK Key 默认仅存在浏览器内存。
- 不记录 Key、完整 Authorization Header 或含 Key 的异常信息。
- 生产日志对请求正文和敏感字段进行脱敏。
- 服务端 Key 模式与 BYOK 模式分别进行权限和配额检查。
- 对 AI 接口设置速率限制、输入输出上限和超时。
- 对不可信客户端不能依赖前端校验作为唯一安全边界。

### 14.4 SSRF 与外部资源

外部图片下载和其他外部资源请求必须防止 SSRF：

- 禁止本机、私有网络、链路本地及云元数据地址。
- 检查 DNS 解析与重定向目标。
- 对目标地址、端口、协议、超时和文件大小设置限制。
- 优先通过允许列表管理外部请求能力。

### 14.5 Prompt Injection

- README、Markdown 和仓库文本只视为数据，不视为系统指令。
- 不允许仓库内容覆盖系统提示词或安全规则。
- AI 生成结果仅进入结构化内容模型。
- AI 不获得命令执行、任意文件访问或部署权限。
- 生成后重新执行 Schema、链接、路径和内容安全审计。

### 14.6 许可证与内容权利

- 未发现许可证不等于允许自由复制或再分发。
- 明确提示用户确认其有权使用和发布所选内容。
- 保存许可证识别来源及用户确认记录。
- 不擅自替换仓库许可证，不生成虚假的授权声明。

## 15. 测试策略

### 15.1 单元测试

- GitHub URL 解析与校验。
- 路径规范化和目录穿越防护。
- Markdown 解析与 HTML 安全处理。
- Zod Schema 校验。
- AI Provider 切换。
- 中文、英文提示词和内容结构校验。
- 图片数量上限与去重。
- LICENSE 检测和警告状态。
- ZIP 文件清单与密钥泄漏检查。

### 15.2 集成测试

- 公开仓库读取。
- README 和相对图片路径解析。
- DeepSeek / MiniMax 适配器。
- 服务端 Key 与 BYOK 两种模式。
- AI 返回无效 JSON 时的错误处理。
- 外部图片下载失败后的回退逻辑。
- 审计报告与确认状态。
- 静态网站生成和 ZIP 打包。

### 15.3 端到端测试

- 输入仓库 URL 并加载预览。
- 切换中文与英文。
- 切换服务端 Key 和 BYOK。
- BYOK 刷新页面后不保留 Key。
- 本地图片超过 50 张时出现清晰提示。
- 未发现 LICENSE 时显示警告，并要求明确确认。
- 阻断项未解决时禁止最终导出。
- 完成审核后成功生成 ZIP。
- ZIP 中不包含 API Key、密钥字段或服务端配置。
- 生成的网站在本地静态服务器和 Netlify 上均能正确打开。

## 16. 验收标准

### P0：基础生成能力

- [ ] 可以输入并校验公开 GitHub 仓库地址。
- [ ] 可以读取仓库元数据、README 和选定文档。
- [ ] 可以生成项目主页与文档导航。
- [ ] 使用固定模板生成网站，不执行仓库代码。
- [ ] 可以导出完整 ZIP。
- [ ] 生成的网站不依赖 RepoSite 后端运行。

### P1：内容预览与审计

- [ ] 可以预览并编辑生成内容。
- [ ] 本地图片复制正确，单次最多处理 50 张。
- [ ] 外部图片默认保留链接。
- [ ] 可以检查内部链接、图片路径和来源。
- [ ] 无 LICENSE 时显示明确警告。
- [ ] 用户必须明确确认后才能在无 LICENSE 状态下继续。
- [ ] 阻断项存在时禁止最终生成。
- [ ] 所有警告都能追溯到对应内容或资源。

### P2：AI 能力

- [ ] 同时支持服务端配置 Key 和用户自带 Key。
- [ ] 支持 DeepSeek、MiniMax。
- [ ] 服务端 Key 不会出现在浏览器资源、日志或 ZIP 中。
- [ ] BYOK Key 不写入 LocalStorage、URL 或 ZIP。
- [ ] 支持中文、英文选择，单次任务只使用一种目标语言。
- [ ] AI 返回内容通过 Schema 校验。
- [ ] 对 AI 推断内容进行明确标记。
- [ ] AI 请求失败时能恢复、重试或退回手动编辑。

### P3：AI 友好输出

- [ ] 输出语义化 HTML 和 Markdown。
- [ ] 输出 `llms.txt`、`sitemap.xml` 和 JSON 内容索引。
- [ ] 生成稳定的页面路由和标题。
- [ ] 保留来源信息和许可证声明。
- [ ] 可使用 Pagefind 等方案提供站内搜索。
- [ ] 不宣称能保证 AI 抓取或训练。

## 17. 开发计划

### Phase 0：项目骨架与安全基线

任务：

- 初始化 Astro、TypeScript、Tailwind CSS 和 shadcn/ui。
- 建立 React 交互组件。
- 配置 Netlify 构建与 Functions。
- 定义数据模型、Zod Schema 和统一错误结构。
- 实现 GitHub URL 校验、路径规范化和基础安全策略。
- 建立 Vitest、Playwright 与 CI。

完成条件：项目可以构建、测试并部署；关键输入校验通过测试。

### Phase 1：GitHub 读取与确定性生成

任务：

- 读取公开仓库元数据、README 和文档。
- 固定生成任务的 commit SHA。
- 实现 Markdown 解析与静态页面模板。
- 实现基础资源复制和相对链接转换。
- 实现 HTML、Markdown 和 JSON 文件输出。
- 实现 ZIP 导出。

完成条件：无需 AI 即可从公开仓库生成一个可部署的静态网站。

### Phase 2：预览、资源处理与审计

任务：

- 建立内容预览与人工编辑界面。
- 实现最多 50 张本地图片的处理限制。
- 实现外部图片保留与可选下载。
- 实现内部链接、图片、来源和许可证审计。
- 实现阻断项与警告项的状态流转。
- 对无 LICENSE 场景实现明确确认流程。

完成条件：未通过阻断级检查不能最终导出；无 LICENSE 可以在用户明确确认后继续。

      return ref.ref.toLowerCase();
    }
    const data = await this.request&lt;Record&lt;string, unknown&gt;&gt;(
      `/repos/${encodeURIComponent(ref.owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(ref.repo)}/commits/${encodeURIComponent(
        ref.ref ?? 'HEAD',
      )}`,
    );
    const sha = typeof data.sha === 'string' ? data.sha : '';
    if (!/^[0-9a-f]{40}$/.test(sha)) {
      throw new RepoSiteError('GITHUB_ERROR', '无法解析提交 SHA。');
    }
    return sha;
  }

  async getTree(ref: RepositoryRef, commitSha: string): Promise&lt;TreeResult&gt; {
    const base = `/repos/${encodeURIComponent(ref.owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(ref.repo)}/git/trees/${commitSha}`;
    const data = await this.request&lt;Record&lt;string, unknown&gt;&gt;(`${base}?recursive=1`);
    let entries = toTreeEntries(data.tree);
    let truncated = Boolean(data.truncated);

    if (truncated) {
      // GitHub truncates very large trees: fall back to per-directory reads.
      const topLevel = entries.filter((entry) =&gt; entry.type === 'tree');
      const merged = new Map(entries.map((entry) =&gt; [entry.path, entry]));
      for (const directory of topLevel.slice(0, 60)) {
        const sub = await this.request&lt;Record&lt;string, unknown&gt;&gt;(
          `${base}/${encodeURIComponent(directory.path)}?recursive=1`,
        );
        for (const entry of toTreeEntries(sub.tree)) {
          if (!merged.has(entry.path)) merged.set(entry.path, entry);
        }
        if (!sub.truncated) truncated = false;
      }
      entries = [...merged.values()];
    }

    return { entries, truncated };
  }

  async getReadme(ref: RepositoryRef, commitSha: string): Promise&lt;ReadmeFile | null&gt; {
    try {
      const data = await this.request&lt;Record&lt;string, unknown&gt;&gt;(
        `/repos/${encodeURIComponent(ref.owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(ref.repo)}/readme?ref=${commitSha}`,
      );
      const content = typeof data.content === 'string' ? data.content : '';
      const encoding = typeof data.encoding === 'string' ? data.encoding : 'base64';
      const path = typeof data.path === 'string' ? data.path : 'README.md';
      if (encoding !== 'base64') {
        throw new RepoSiteError('GITHUB_ERROR', '不支持的 README 编码。');
      }
      return { path, content: decodeBase64(content) };
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof RepoSiteError &amp;&amp; error.code === 'GITHUB_ERROR' &amp;&amp; error.status === 404) {
        return null;
      }
      throw error;
    }
  }

  async getBlobBytes(ref: RepositoryRef, blobSha: string): Promise&lt;Uint8Array&gt; {
    const data = await this.request&lt;Record&lt;string, unknown&gt;&gt;(
      `/repos/${encodeURIComponent(ref.owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(ref.repo)}/git/blobs/${blobSha}`,
    );
    const content = typeof data.content === 'string' ? data.content : '';
    const encoding = typeof data.encoding === 'string' ? data.encoding : 'base64';
    if (encoding === 'utf-8') {
      return new TextEncoder().encode(content);
    }
    if (encoding !== 'base64') {
      throw new RepoSiteError('GITHUB_ERROR', '不支持的文件编码。');
    }
    return base64ToBytes(content);
  }

  private async request&lt;T&gt;(path: string): Promise&lt;T&gt; {
    const url = `${GITHUB_API_ORIGIN}${path}`;
    let lastError: unknown;

    for (let attempt = 0; attempt &lt;= this.maxRetries; attempt += 1) {
      const controller = new AbortController();
      const timer = setTimeout(() =&gt; controller.abort(), this.requestTimeoutMs);
      try {
        const headers: Record&lt;string, string&gt; = {
          Accept: 'application/vnd.github+json',
          'X-GitHub-Api-Version': '2022-11-28',
          'User-Agent': this.userAgent,
        };
        if (this.token) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${this.token}`;

        const response = await this.fetchImpl(url, { headers, signal: controller.signal });
        const status = response.status;
        if (status === 404) {
          throw new RepoSiteError('GITHUB_ERROR', 'GitHub 未找到该资源（仓库可能不存在或不是公开仓库）。', {
            status: 404,
          });
        }
        if (status === 401 || status === 403 || status === 429) {
          const remaining = response.headers.get('x-ratelimit-remaining');
          if (remaining === '0' || status === 429) {
            throw new RepoSiteError('GITHUB_RATE_LIMIT', 'GitHub API 调用频率超限，请稍后重试。');</message>

import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import JSZip from 'jszip';

const SHA = 'a'.repeat(40);
const README = '# Demo Project\n\nDemo readme with an image.\n\n![logo](assets/logo.png)\n\nSee the [guide](docs/guide.md).\n';

import { mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { loadRepository, auditRepository } from '@/lib/pipeline';
import { generateSite } from '@/lib/site/generator';
import { packageSite } from '@/lib/export/zip';

const REPO = 'https://github.com/vitejs/vite';

describe('repository integration', () =&gt; {
  it('loads, audits and generates a site for a public repository', async () =&gt; {
    const content = await loadRepository(REPO, {
      targetLanguage: 'zh-CN',
      maxDocuments: 4,
      maxImages: 4,
    });
    expect(content.metadata.sourceCommitSha).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{40}$/);
    expect(content.documents.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(content.documents[0]!.slug).toBe('readme');

    const report = await auditRepository(content);
    if (report.blockingCount &gt; 0) {
      console.log(JSON.stringify(report.issues.filter((i) =&gt; i.severity === 'blocking'), null, 1));
    }
    expect(report.blockingCount).toBe(0);

    const site = await generateSite({
      metadata: content.metadata,
      documents: content.documents,
      assets: content.assets,
      binaryAssets: content.binaryAssets,
      language: 'zh-CN',
    });
    const paths = site.files.map((f) =&gt; f.path);
    expect(paths).toContain('index.html');
    expect(paths).toContain('llms.txt');
    expect(paths).toContain('llms-full.txt');
    expect(paths).toContain('sitemap.xml');
    expect(paths).toContain('ai/manifest.json');
    expect(paths).toContain('docs/readme.html');

    const zip = await packageSite(site.files);
    expect(zip.fileCount).toBe(site.files.length);
    expect(zip.fileNames).toContain('index.html');

    const outDir = new URL('../.tmp/generated-site/', import.meta.url);
    await mkdir(outDir, { recursive: true });
    for (const file of site.files) {
      const target = new URL(file.path, outDir);
      await mkdir(new URL('.', target), { recursive: true });
      await writeFile(target, typeof file.content === 'string' ? file.content : Buffer.from(file.content));
    }
  }, 120_000);
});</message>

<message type="tool">/**
 * @typedef {import('hast').Root} Root
 */

/**
 * @typedef Options
 *   Configuration (optional).
 * @property {string} [prefix='']
 *   Prefix to add in front of `id`s (default: `''`).
 */

import GithubSlugger from 'github-slugger'
import {headingRank} from 'hast-util-heading-rank'
import {toString} from 'hast-util-to-string'
import {visit} from 'unist-util-visit'

/** @type {Options} */
const emptyOptions = {}
const slugs = new GithubSlugger()

/**
 * Add `id`s to headings.
 *
 * @param {Options | null | undefined} [options]
 *   Configuration (optional).
 * @returns
 *   Transform.
 */
export default function rehypeSlug(options) {
  const settings = options || emptyOptions
  const prefix = settings.prefix || ''

  /**
   * @param {Root} tree
   *   Tree.
   * @returns {undefined}
   *   Nothing.
   */
  return function (tree) {
    slugs.reset()

    visit(tree, 'element', function (node) {
      if (headingRank(node) &amp;&amp; !node.properties.id) {
        node.properties.id = prefix + slugs.slug(toString(node))
      }
    })
  }
}
